--- Assumptions / constants (must match task spec) ---\n    patients_per_therapy = 240\n    doses_per_fill_30 = 60\n    doses_per_fill_90 = 180\n    fills_per_year_30 = 12\n    fills_per_year_90 = 4\n\n    # --- Load inputs ---\n    acquisition_rows = read_csv_rows(ACQUISITION_COST_CSV)\n    packaging_rows = read_csv_rows(PACKAGING_COST_CSV)\n    reimbursement_rows = read_csv_rows(REIMBURSEMENT_CSV)\n\n    # --- Normalize/join keys ---\n    # acquisition_cost.csv: therapy, price_per_1000_doses_usd, canister_size_units\n    acquisition_by_therapy = {}\n    for r in acquisition_rows:\n        therapy = (r.get(\"therapy\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not therapy:\n            continue\n        acquisition_by_therapy[therapy] = {\n            \"therapy\": therapy,\n            \"price_per_1000_doses_usd\": d(r[\"price_per_1000_doses_usd\"]),\n            \"canister_size_units\": int(str(r[\"canister_size_units\"]).strip()),\n        }\n\n    # packaging_cost.csv: canister_size_units, packaging_cost_usd\n    packaging_by_size = {}\n    for r in packaging_rows:\n        size_raw = (r.get(\"canister_size_units\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not size_raw:\n            continue\n        size = int(size_raw)\n        packaging_by_size[size] = d(r[\"packaging_cost_usd\"])\n\n    # reimbursement.csv: therapy, reimbursement_per_fill_240_patients_usd\n    reimbursement_by_therapy = {}\n    for r in reimbursement_rows:\n        therapy = (r.get(\"therapy\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not therapy:\n            continue\n        reimbursement_by_therapy[therapy] = d(r[\"reimbursement_per_fill_240_patients_usd\"])\n\n    # --- Merge ---\n    missing_reimb = []\n    missing_packaging = []\n\n    therapies_out = []\n\n    for therapy in sorted(acquisition_by_therapy.keys()):\n        acq = acquisition_by_therapy[therapy]\n        canister_size_units = acq[\"canister_size_units\"]\n\n        if therapy not in reimbursement_by_therapy:\n            missing_reimb.append(therapy)\n            continue\n        if canister_size_units not in packaging_by_size:\n            missing_packaging.append({\n                \"therapy\": therapy,\n                \"canister_size_units\": canister_size_units,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        price_per_1000 = acq[\"price_per_1000_doses_usd\"]\n        packaging_cost_usd = packaging_by_size[canister_size_units]\n        reimbursement_per_fill_240 = reimbursement_by_therapy[therapy]\n\n        # Drug cost per fill is per-patient per fill.\n        drug_cost_per_fill_30 = (Decimal(doses_per_fill_30) * price_per_1000) / Decimal(1000)\n        drug_cost_per_fill_90 = (Decimal(doses_per_fill_90) * price_per_1000) / Decimal(1000)\n\n        # Annual drug cost for the cohort.\n        annual_drug_cost_30 = drug_cost_per_fill_30 * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_drug_cost_90 = drug_cost_per_fill_90 * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        # Sanity check per instructions: annual drug cost should be identical\n        # under these assumptions (same annual doses implied by fill sizes/frequency).\n        if q2(annual_drug_cost_30) != q2(annual_drug_cost_90):\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Annual drug cost mismatch for {therapy}: \"\n                f\"30-day={q2(annual_drug_cost_30)} vs 90-day={q2(annual_drug_cost_90)}\"\n            )\n\n        # Packaging cost is per patient per fill.\n        annual_packaging_cost_30 = packaging_cost_usd * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_packaging_cost_90 = packaging_cost_usd * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        # Reimbursement is per fill for all 240 patients.\n        annual_reimbursement_30 = reimbursement_per_fill_240 * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_reimbursement_90 = reimbursement_per_fill_240 * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        annual_margin_30 = annual_reimbursement_30 - annual_drug_cost_30 - annual_packaging_cost_30\n        annual_margin_90 = annual_reimbursement_90 - annual_drug_cost_90 - annual_packaging_cost_90\n\n        margin_difference_90_minus_30 = annual_margin_90 - annual_margin_30\n\n        therapies_out.append({\n            \"therapy\": therapy,\n            \"canister_size_units\": canister_size_units,\n            \"price_per_1000_doses_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(price_per_1000),\n            \"packaging_cost_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(packaging_cost_usd),\n            \"reimbursement_per_fill_240_patients_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(reimbursement_per_fill_240),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_drug_cost_30),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_drug_cost_90),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_packaging_cost_30),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_packaging_cost_90),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_reimbursement_30),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_reimbursement_90),\n            \"annual_margin_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_margin_30),\n            \"annual_margin_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_margin_90),\n            \"margin_difference_90_minus_30_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(margin_difference_90_minus_30),\n        })\n\n    if missing_reimb:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing reimbursement rows for therapies: {missing_reimb}\")\n    if missing_packaging:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing packaging rows for therapies/sizes: {missing_packaging}\")\n\n    # Ensure totals are rounded to 2dp as well.\n    total_annual_margin_30_q = q2(total_annual_margin_30)\n    total_annual_margin_90_q = q2(total_annual_margin_90)\n    total_diff_q = q2(total_diff)\n    absolute_total_diff_q = q2(absolute_total_diff)\n\n    # --- Decision rule ---\n    if absolute_total_diff_q < Decimal(\"12000\"):\n        decision = \"adopt_90_day\"\n    else:\n        decision = \"keep_30_day\"\n\n    recommendation = {\n        \"decision\": decision,\n        \"justification\": (\n            f\"Absolute total annual margin difference is ${float(absolute_total_diff_q):,.2f}; \"\n            f\"threshold is $12,000.00.\"\n        ),\n    }\n\n    out_obj = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"patients_per_therapy\": patients_per_therapy,\n            \"doses_per_fill_30\": doses_per_fill_30,\n            \"doses_per_fill_90\": doses_per_fill_90,\n            \"fills_per_year_30\": fills_per_year_30,\n            \"fills_per_year_90\": fills_per_year_90,\n        },\n        \"therapies\": therapies_out,  # Drug cost per fill is per-patient per fill.\n        drug_cost_per_fill_30 = (Decimal(doses_per_fill_30) * price_per_1000) / Decimal(1000)\n        drug_cost_per_fill_90 = (Decimal(doses_per_fill_90) * price_per_1000) / Decimal(1000)\n\n        # Annual drug cost for the cohort.\n        annual_drug_cost_30 = drug_cost_per_fill_30 * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_drug_cost_90 = drug_cost_per_fill_90 * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        # Sanity check per instructions: annual drug cost should be identical\n        # under these assumptions (same annual doses implied by fill sizes/frequency).\n        if q2(annual_drug_cost_30) != q2(annual_drug_cost_90):\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Annual drug cost mismatch for {therapy}: \"\n                f\"30-day={q2(annual_drug_cost_30)} vs 90-day={q2(annual_drug_cost_90)}\"\n            )\n\n        # Packaging cost is per patient per fill.\n        annual_packaging_cost_30 = packaging_cost_usd * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_packaging_cost_90 = packaging_cost_usd * Decimal(patients_per_therapy) * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        # Reimbursement is per fill for all 240 patients.\n        annual_reimbursement_30 = reimbursement_per_fill_240 * Decimal(fills_per_year_30)\n        annual_reimbursement_90 = reimbursement_per_fill_240 * Decimal(fills_per_year_90)\n\n        annual_margin_30 = annual_reimbursement_30 - annual_drug_cost_30 - annual_packaging_cost_30\n        annual_margin_90 = annual_reimbursement_90 - annual_drug_cost_90 - annual_packaging_cost_90\n\n        margin_difference_90_minus_30 = annual_margin_90 - annual_margin_30\n\n        therapies_out.append({\n            \"therapy\": therapy,\n            \"canister_size_units\": canister_size_units,\n            \"price_per_1000_doses_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(price_per_1000),\n            \"packaging_cost_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(packaging_cost_usd),\n            \"reimbursement_per_fill_240_patients_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(reimbursement_per_fill_240),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_drug_cost_30),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_drug_cost_90),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_packaging_cost_30),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_packaging_cost_90),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_reimbursement_30),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_reimbursement_90),\n            \"annual_margin_30_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_margin_30),\n            \"annual_margin_90_day_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(annual_margin_90),\n            \"margin_difference_90_minus_30_usd\": dec_to_float_2dp(margin_difference_90_minus_30),\n        })\n\n    if missing_reimb:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing reimbursement rows for therapies: {missing_reimb}\")\n    if missing_packaging:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing packaging rows for therapies/sizes: {missing_packaging}\")\n\n    # --- Totals ---\n    total_annual_margin_30 = sum(Decimal(str(t[\"annual_margin_30_day_usd\"])) for t in therapies_out)\n    total_annual_margin_90 = sum(Decimal(str(t[\"annual_margin_90_day_usd\"])) for t in therapies_out)\n\n    total_diff = sum(Decimal(str(t[\"margin_difference_90_minus_30_usd\"])) for t in therapies_out)\n    absolute_total_diff = abs(total_diff)\n\n    # Ensure totals are rounded to 2dp as well.\n    total_annual_margin_30_q = q2(total_annual_margin_30)\n    total_annual_margin_90_q = q2(total_annual_margin_90)\n    total_diff_q = q2(total_diff)\n    absolute_total_diff_q = q2(absolute_total_diff)\n\n    # --- Decision rule ---\n    if absolute_total_diff_q < Decimal(\"12000\"):\n        decision = \"adopt_90_day\"\n    else:\n        decision = \"keep_30_day\"\n\n    recommendation = {\n        \"decision\": decision,\n        \"justification\": (\n            f\"Absolute total annual margin difference is ${float(absolute_total_diff_q):,.2f}; \"\n            f\"threshold is $12,000.00.\"\n        ),\n    }\n\n    out_obj = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"patients_per_therapy\": patients_per_therapy,\n            \"doses_per_fill_30\": doses_per_fill_30,\n            \"doses_per_fill_90\": doses_per_fill_90,\n            \"fills_per_year_30\": fills_per_year_30,\n            \"fills_per_year_90\": fills_per_year_90,\n        },\n        \"therapies\": therapies_out,  #
